**Ledger flips (1f49f45).** CV-43 and RP-13 FIXED at ee7b6e0: CI run 41976819752 on 2952958 ran the Dist job's `Run pnpm check:snippets` step green, which was the one clause the wave-1 judge left open on both rows (wave1-150-extras3.json through ledger-apply-wave.mjs).

**CI on the pushed head 111c351, and a process rule.** Run 41982245737 (06:42Z to 06:45Z) FAILED on two things, both W2-06 artefacts that anticipated W2-03's then-uncommitted code: the dry-run diff test in tests/unit/codemod-cli.test.ts expects the single-quoted import that only import-rewrite's printOptions print produces (HEAD's `root.toSource()` printed double quotes), and check:package's file list names dist/codemod/chunk-jscodeshift.mjs and chunk-print-options.mjs, which rolldown splits out only when a transform imports print-options.js and jscodeshift. A throwaway worktree at 111c351 with its own offline install (run dir head-probe-111c351.log) reproduced both: its dist/codemod holds chunk-worker.mjs, cli.mjs, worker.mjs and the transforms, and the test fails 1 of 62. Neither expectation was weakened; W2-03 (58302f4) supplies the code, and the chunk set is re-checked with `pnpm build && pnpm check:package` before the next push. Rule from here: a partial-wave commit set is verified at HEAD, away from the shared checkout's uncommitted sibling work, before any push (scratchpad head-check.sh: worktree at HEAD, offline install, vitest, removed; `git worktree remove` fails with 'Filename too long' on the worktree's node_modules, so the script deletes with rm -rf and prunes). After the landing the pre-push gates ran on the clean tree at 07:40:50Z (run dir push-150-w2-final.log): Biome 313 files in 106 ms, the typecheck script over modern-cmdk, create-modern-cmdk, tests, tests/e2e and tests/docs, size-limit with all seven entries under budget; lefthook's pre-push hook then built the packages and ran vitest and size-limit again (its summary: done in 11.94 seconds, build 0.59, test 10.44, size 0.21); the push landed 111c351..ffe6bb1; CI run 41988965827 on ffe6bb1 ran 07:41:07Z to 07:45:11Z and completed SUCCESS (ci-watch-ffe6bb1.log), the dry-run diff test and the tarball file list green with W2-03's code in place. The pre-commit hook was never bypassed: commit-wave.mjs runs a plain git commit -F, lefthook 2.2.2 answers git hook run pre-commit, and pnpm typecheck takes 2.3 s under TypeScript 7's native compiler, which is why the four landing commits took about three seconds each (commit-w2-final.log step timestamps, 07:39:10Z to 07:39:22Z).

**Judge** (ledger-dispositions-150-wave2, run wf_761cd6e6-377: 3 opus-verifier groups, 431K subagent tokens, 6 min 26 s, judged at HEAD ffe6bb1 with the working tree equal to HEAD apart from the owner's untracked AGENTS.md). 66 rows over the eight items: FIXED 62, PARTIAL 4, OPEN 0, REFUTED 0; per item W2-01 20 FIXED, W2-02 14, W2-03 8, W2-04 7, W2-05 7 FIXED and 4 PARTIAL, W2-06 3, W2-07 1, W2-08 2. The four PARTIALs: DT-07, F31 and RP-23 on one sentence, packages/create-modern-cmdk/README.md:75 still said dependencies are declared as caret ranges of known-good versions while fab60a5 writes exact pins (W2-05 never touched the README); CV-25 because templates/shared/src/main.tsx and shared/vite.config.ts sit in no tsconfig (tests/tsconfig.json includes only */src/App.tsx), which the judge deferred to W4-04's scaffold tsc. Its probes: the built dist/codemod/cli.mjs data-attrs --dry-run over a scratch project holding .css, .scss, JSX in .js, .json and edge selectors (every file migrated as specified, the .json skipped, one report, exit 0); vitest on the three transform suites 333 of 333 and on data-attrs 59 of 59; the create, pool, submodule, files, snippet, manifest and dist suites re-run singly, all green; pnpm view dist-tags.latest for the eight scaffold pins, each equal to versions.ts today; ATTR_MAP and UNMAPPED_ATTRS against cmdk 1.1.1's dist (13 rendered names, matched); import-rewrite --dry-run at concurrency 4 and 1 byte-identical. Two corrections of record from the judge: the fab60a5 commit message says the templates import modern-cmdk/react/styles.css while the code imports modern-cmdk/styles.css, the correct export; and RP-24's Command-root ref question belongs to RA-32, not to forward-ref. It proposed eight extra rows: three were documentation lines the wave's code had made false, fixed in 4ad3040 instead of filed (the README sentence above; data-attrs.ts's header now lists .mts and .cts in the script pass, which files.ts routes to the ts parser; forward-ref.ts's header now says a props spread in any single-parameter render function, typed or untyped, forwards the caller's ref, the typed form without a ref type argument reported, the untyped form unwrapped without a report), and five are filed below.

The new package builds dist/{cli,worker}.mjs, the print-options and worker chunks and transforms/*.mjs (8 files, 91.57 kB), bundles nothing but its own source (tsdown deps.neverBundle jscodeshift, onlyBundle [], onlyImport jscodeshift: the worker's probe with a stray `import 'ast-types'` failed the build with "ast-types is imported in chunk-print-options.mjs but is not included in deps.onlyImport option"), declares jscodeshift 17.4.0 as its one runtime dependency through `catalog:` (the verifier's `pnpm pack` shows 17.4.0 in the packed manifest), runs strict by default, carries a short true README stub (W7-02 writes the real one), MIT LICENSE, version 0.0.0 until changesets names it (W8-01), engines.node >=26.4.0. modern-cmdk keeps the codemod it ships until 2.0: tsdown.config.ts bundles ../modern-cmdk-codemod/src into dist/codemod/cli.mjs with deps.alwaysBundle jscodeshift, an explicit deps.onlyBundle list of ten packages (dropping tslib from the list fails the build: "tslib is located in node_modules but is not included in deps.onlyBundle option") and deps.onlyImport [] (node: builtins only), so the tarball needs no runtime dependency; its `bin` is dist/codemod/bin.mjs, the deprecated `modern-cmdk` shim (897 B): one deprecation line on stderr, --lenient put first unless argv carries --strict (the 1.x exit codes), the bundled cli.mjs run with program 'modern-cmdk'. **Ruling: recast stays 0.23.21, held (peak-modern §1).** The brief said: if W1-01's probe shows recast 0.24.0 keeps what printOptions relies on, add a pnpm override and run the whole fixture suite on it. The probe did show that, so the brief's condition picked the override; the worker chose a hold instead and did not run the suite on 0.24.0, and the verifier flagged the deviation as its first minor. The hold stands on a reason the brief had not weighed: jscodeshift 17.4.0 declares recast ^0.23.11 (pnpm view, 2026-11-05; latest is 0.24.0), and a pnpm override never reaches modern-cmdk-codemod's published manifest, so consumers would run recast 0.23.x while this repository's fixtures ran 0.24.0. A test suite that proves a version no consumer gets proves the wrong thing. .peak-modern-holds.json carries the entry (kind npm, name recast, pin 0.23.21, scoped to the lockfile) with that evidence; it is re-tested when jscodeshift's range moves. `pnpm why recast`: one version, 0.23.21, through jscodeshift 17.4.0 and @types/jscodeshift 17.3.0.

**W3-01b (e7bf0ce): the CLI's default profile follows the package it ships in.** The verifier's second minor: packages/modern-cmdk/dist/codemod/cli.mjs, run directly, used the strict modern-cmdk-codemod profile (Usage: modern-cmdk-codemod) while --version printed modern-cmdk 1.2.3, since only the shim passed program 'modern-cmdk'. Ruling: without CliDeps.program the profile follows the nearest package.json, which run() already located for --version (findPackageJSON from node:module); readNameAndVersion became readPackage, read once when no program is given; a package named modern-cmdk gives the lenient 'modern-cmdk' profile, anything else 'modern-cmdk-codemod'; the shim keeps passing 'modern-cmdk' explicitly (its contract; it needs no package.json, which the no-node_modules copy test relies on). The header and the CliDeps.program doc comment state the rule. Applied by a sonnet-worker through the Agent tool (92 s): tests/dist/cli-smoke.test.ts gains the bundled file as a lenient bin and two W3-01b titles that read both package.json files; 2 red first of 23 with cli.ts untouched, 23 green after the fix and a rebuild of both packages. **CI on 1223dc5 (run 41996249194, 08:40Z to 08:44Z): FAILED, one cause, fixed by W3-01d (2b36297).** Built-package tests: tests/dist/manifest.test.ts:120 expects dist/codemod/bin.mjs to carry an execute bit off Windows and found none on the ubuntu runner. The build did set it: the Build job's log reads "packages/modern-cmdk build: ℹ Granting execute permission to dist/codemod/bin.mjs" (and to cli.mjs, the codemod package's cli.mjs and create's dist/index.mjs; tsdown 0.23.5's ShebangPlugin chmods 0o755 in writeBundle for every entry chunk that starts with a shebang). The bit was lost between jobs: actions/upload-artifact's README ("Permission Loss", read 2026-11-05) says "File permissions are not maintained during zipped artifact upload. All directories will have `755` and all files will have `644`", and six jobs consume the dist artifact, the package job among them, so the published tarballs would have carried 644 bins as well (npm's bin links would still have made them executable on install). W3-01d: the Build job runs `tar -cf "$RUNNER_TEMP/dist.tar" packages/*/dist` and uploads that one file as the dist artifact (same pinned action, name, if-no-files-found and retention); dist-tests, e2e, size, bench, package and consumers download to runner.temp and run `tar -xf "$RUNNER_TEMP/dist.tar"` from the repo root; the node24-install job's tarballs artifact is untouched; the two comments that described the old shape and manifest.test.ts:119-120 state both facts. The README pairs tarring with `archive: false`; left at the default (a zip around the tar, unpacked by download-artifact) for W5-01 to weigh. The wave-4 and wave-5 briefs that touch ci.yml or release.yml (W4-02, W4-05, W5-01) and W4-01 carry a SINCE WAVE 3 paragraph with the tar shape, the package layout, the profile rule and the git resolution; W4-02 gains a tar-header mode check on every bin target when the tarball was packed on POSIX, which pins the chain end to end. **Security review of the 1223dc5 push, and W3-01c (cd9d415; SEC-57).** The automated review flagged files.ts (CWE-426, untrusted search path, MEDIUM): execFile('git', args, { cwd }) with the migrated repository as cwd. Ruling: resolve git to an absolute path from PATH alone, skipping empty and relative entries and any entry inside the target tree, and spawn that path (libuv searches nothing for an absolute path); the review's `-c core.fsmonitor=false` suggestion does not apply (a clone never carries the remote's .git/config). The worker's probe narrowed the finding honestly before changing anything: on this host (libuv 1.52.1, Node 28 nightly) a git.exe planted in the spawn cwd did not run (the real git answered, also when the process itself ran from that directory), while PATH set to `.;...` ran the planted copy, and an absolute PATH entry inside the tree would too. files.ts gained gitSearchDirs(pathValue, root) and resolveGit(root) (git.exe then git.com on win32, git elsewhere; fs.access X_OK, an existence check on Windows; memoized per root and PATH value; ENOENT naming the root when nothing is found, so locateRepo still maps a missing git to no-git); runGit spawns the result. tests/unit/codemod-files.test.ts, SEC-57 titles: nine gitSearchDirs rows, a planted root git (a regression guard: green on HEAD, matching the probe), a relative PATH entry (red on HEAD), an in-tree PATH entry, the ENOENT message; 12 red first of 58, then the files, submodule and CLI suites 124 passed, both packages' tsc and tests exit 0, Biome clean, the dist project 57 passed after a rebuild, the codemod CLI dry-run over the import-rewrite fixtures still "23 changed, 29 unchanged, 0 errors, 45 reported" (run dir w301c-gate.log; sonnet-worker, 172 s).

**CI on 2b36297 (run 41998415732, 08:57Z to 09:01Z): FAILED, four causes.** (1) Test on macos-26 and windows-2025, both Node lines (ubuntu passed): 'SEC-57: a PATH entry inside the repository is skipped and the real git answers' ran the planted copy during `git ls-files` ("git ls-files failed (exit 1): ... Cannot find module '/private/var/folders/.../ls-files'", and the long Temp path on Windows): locateRepo filtered PATH against the raw cwd (`/var/folders/...`, tmpdir() is a symlink on macOS; the runner's short TEMP path on Windows) and gitlinksOf against the realpath toplevel git prints, so the entry read as outside. Fixed by W3-01e, below. (2) Rust & WASM: wasm-pack failed to download binaryen version_130 from github.com (a GitHub 504 at 08:58Z; transient, nothing in the tree; green again on cdc05f2). (3) Links: lychee got a 504 Gateway Timeout from github.com on the repository's own issues URL (CONTRIBUTING.md:853; the same minute; transient). Both fragilities are now in W5-01's brief: install wasm-opt from a pinned binaryen release into a cached path instead of downloading on the hot path, and give lychee a retry budget for 5xx with the repository's own github.com URLs remapped or excluded. (4) Bundle Size: `pnpm size-limit --json` exited 1 with its report swallowed by the redirect; reproduced locally (run dir size-2b36297.json): modern-cmdk-codemod's own files 93,739 B against the 92,000 B budget, the SEC-57 code on a budget set 0.43 kB above W3-01's measurement. Ruled below.

**Security review of the cd9d415 push, and W3-01e (0a41f02; SEC-58).** The second review rated the residual HIGH (CWE-426): the trust boundary was the spawn cwd, so a subdirectory cwd or a submodule owner (ignoredIn passes the submodule directory) left the rest of the work tree searchable; PATH entries and candidates were not realpath-compared; a directory named git passed access. The same realpath gap is cause (1) of the CI failure above, so one fix closes both. Ruling: one trust boundary per operation, `trustBoundary(cwd)` = the realpath of the nearest directory from realpath(cwd) upward that holds a `.git` entry (what `rev-parse --show-toplevel` reports, computed without spawning git), else realpath(cwd); the nearest rather than the review's "outermost", because a dotfiles repository at the user's home would turn every PATH entry under the home (scoop shims, %LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\Git\cmd, ~/.local/bin) into an excluded one and leave the user with no git. discoverFiles (strict profile) and findDirtyFiles compute it once and pass it to every runGit of the operation (rev-parse, status, ls-files, check-ignore in a submodule owner): cwd is where git runs, boundary is what PATH is filtered against. inside() holds the one containment test; gitSearchDirs stays the pure syntactic filter; resolveGit realpaths each kept entry (a dangling entry is skipped) and re-tests it; isGitOutside requires a regular file, X_OK and a realpath outside the boundary. Messages unchanged; the header and the doc comments state the rule and its reasons. Tests (SEC-58 titles; the SEC-57 titles stay): trustBoundary from a subdirectory, from a tree with no .git and through a symlink alias; a planted git in <repo>/bin with cwd a subdirectory; a PATH entry through a symlink alias of the repository; the CI shape (cwd the alias, so the raw cwd and git's toplevel differ; a junction on Windows, which needs no privilege); a directory named git on an outside entry; a planted git in the superproject's bin while check-ignore runs in the submodule (codemod-submodule.test.ts). 9 red first of 69 with files.ts untouched; then the files, submodule and CLI suites 132 passed, the CI command 1224 passed and 1 skipped, both packages' tsc and tests exit 0, Biome clean, the dist project 57 passed after a rebuild, the dry-run unchanged (sonnet-worker, 204 s); the orchestrator's gate on the merged tree: vitest 1281 passed and 1 skipped in 65 files, pnpm typecheck exit 0, Biome clean on the five touched files (run dir w301e-gate.log). The worker's one residual stands as written: trustBoundary realpaths cwd, so a nonexistent cwd now rejects ENOENT from discoverFiles or findDirtyFiles instead of reading as a missing git; cwd is process.cwd(). Severity: the review's HIGH is recorded; the ledger rates SEC-58 medium, since every path needs an absolute PATH entry inside the migrated tree or a link to it.

**Ruling: the modern-cmdk-codemod own-files budget is 100 kB (cdc05f2).** W3-01 set it at 92 kB over a 91.57 kB measurement; the SEC-57 code took the files to 93,739 B and the SEC-58 code to 96,307 B (`pnpm size-limit --json`, run dir size-w301e.json). The budget exists to catch a dependency bundled by mistake (jscodeshift's chunk alone is 3.05 MB in the modern-cmdk bin) and silent growth, not to refuse a security fix: 100 kB still fails on any bundled dependency and leaves 3.7 kB for 1.5.0's remaining codemod work. The other eight budgets are unchanged (core 6,399 of 6,500 B; react 5,558 of 5,600; dialog 1,422 of 1,500; CommandSeparator 4,830 of 5,000; core + react 10,019 of 10,100; styles.css 3,959 of 4,000; the codemod bin 3,148,577 of 3,400,000; the shim 897 of 1,000).

**Ruling: the pre-push size job's glob (a881dde; DT-67).** The pushes of 2b36297 and cdc05f2 both printed "verify ❯ size (skip) no matching push files" although each changed packages/modern-cmdk-codemod/src/files.ts: lefthook's glob `packages/*/src/**/*.{ts,tsx}` needs a directory between `src/` and `**/`, so files directly under a package's src never matched, and the budget failure above reached CI instead of the hook. Probed with `lefthook run pre-push --job size --file <path>`: files.ts skipped, codemod/bin.ts ran. The glob is now `{packages/*/src/**,packages/*/tsdown.config.ts,package.json}` (any file under a package's src, a tsdown config, or the root package.json where the budgets live), probed on all four shapes (files.ts, codemod/bin.ts and package.json run size-limit; apps/docs/guide/installation.md skips); the comment states the rule and the reason.

The failed job was re-run at 09:19Z: attempt 2 completed at 09:20:46Z with every job green (run 42000484240 SUCCESS; run dir ci-watch-cdc05f2-rerun.log), so the tree at cdc05f2 is proven end to end: Built-package tests with the 755 bins out of the tar, the four Test matrices with the SEC-57 and SEC-58 titles, Rust & WASM, Bundle Size under the 100 kB budget, Links.

**Security review of the 0a41f02 push, and W3-01f (d52d6f2; SEC-59).** The third review (CWE-426, MEDIUM) named the one case the nearest-work-tree rule gets wrong: with cwd inside a submodule checkout the nearest `.git` is the submodule's `.git` file (`gitdir: ../.git/modules/<name>`), so the boundary was the submodule and the superproject around it, cloned from the same untrusted source, stayed searchable (a planted git at <super>/bin on PATH ran for a dirty check from <super>/inner: red first). Ruling: trustBoundary climbs from a submodule to the superproject that holds the `.git/modules` directory its gitdir target lies under (superprojectOf takes the last `.git`/`modules` pair of the realpath; superprojectAbove reads the file, matches `/^gitdir:\s*(.+?)\s*$/m`, resolves against the submodule and realpaths; any read, match or realpath failure stops at the submodule), and again while that superproject is itself a submodule; a `.git` directory (an ordinary repository, a nested one included) stops the climb, which keeps the dotfiles-at-home exception; a linked worktree's `.git` file (`.git/worktrees`) stops it, since the main repository of a worktree is the user's own. The review's two other suggestions are declined: validating that `.git` holds a HEAD (git refuses to add any path component named .git, so a clone cannot carry a decoy `.git`; a dependency's install script that could plant one runs arbitrary code already), and dropping every node_modules/.bin PATH entry wherever it sits (the boundary already covers the clone's own node_modules, and the resolver's rule stays one sentence: PATH outside the migrated tree). Tests (SEC-59 titles in codemod-submodule.test.ts, with plantGit and scratchRepo helpers): inner and inner/deep bound to realpath(super); super > mid > leaf climbs to super; a planted git at super/bin with findDirtyFiles run from super/inner reads clean; a linked worktree and an ordinary nested repository are their own boundary. 3 red first of 74 with files.ts untouched; then the files, submodule and CLI suites 137 passed, the CI command 1229 passed and 1 skipped, both packages' tsc and tests exit 0, Biome clean, the dist project 57 passed after a rebuild, the dry-run unchanged, own files 97.92 kB of the 100 kB budget (sonnet-worker, 179 s); the orchestrator's gate on the merged tree: vitest 1286 passed and 1 skipped in 65 files, pnpm typecheck exit 0, Biome clean (run dir w301f-gate.log). The worker's one note stands: gitlinksOf still lists one submodule level, as its comment says (SEC-56, 1.6.0).

**Ruling 1, SEC-60: the first-publish job may not both run npm-resolved code and hold the signing credential.** The W4-05 verifier (important) found that round 1's first-publish-tarball held id-token: write and attestations: write while its smoke step npm-installed the packed codemod without a lockfile and executed the installed bin, so any transitive package could have attested arbitrary artifacts under this repository's identity. Ruling: split the job. first-publish-tarball keeps every step up to the upload with contents: read only and exposes state, tarball and sha256 outputs from a Hash step; first-publish-attest (needs it; state first; contents: read, id-token: write, attestations: write, artifact-metadata: write) downloads the artifact, refuses anything but exactly one tarball whose name and sha256 equal those outputs, unpacks it for syft, attests provenance and SBOM with the same pinned actions, uploads the SPDX file as first-publish-modern-cmdk-codemod-sbom, and runs no setup-node, pnpm, npm or repository script and no checkout (the actions/attest README mentions checkout only in its build examples; the anchore/sbom-action README never). Accepted deviations: artifact-metadata: write stays because the moved attest steps hold it today; the per-step ifs became the job if; the extra SBOM upload keeps the SPDX the owner used to get from one artifact. The header now names attest, first-publish-attest and publish as the only id-token: write jobs and says why the smoke job holds none; the version-job comment agrees with the scope gate (apply-release-plan 8.1.1 dist/index.mjs:180 skips workspace:*). The layout on disk after download-artifact, and attest and sbom-action without a checkout, are proven only by the owner's release run (W8-01); owner-gated-1.5.0.md section 1 now downloads both artifacts, checks the sha256 file, verifies both attestations and reads source.txt and the resolved tree.

**Ruling 2: the flow licence text and the noticeFiles key.** flow-estree@0.337.0 and flow-parser@0.337.0 (bundled through jscodeshift) ship README.md, package.json and dist only, no licence file; both declare MIT and the facebook/flow repository. The generator must neither skip them (MIT redistribution needs the notice) nor fetch at generation time (offline, deterministic). Ruling: pin the facebook/flow LICENSE (MIT, "Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates."; last commit 741104e69c43057ebd32804dd6bcc1b5e97548ea of 2021-12-30; fetched 2026-11-05 through the GitHub API; 1088 bytes, LF, sha256 da6d3703ed11cbe42bd212c725957c98da23cbff1998c05fa4b3d976d1a58e93) as tests/package/notices/flow-0.337.0-LICENSE.txt, named by two pins (package, version, file, source blob URL, fetched, reason; all required) and rendered with one provenance line above the verbatim text. The worker put the pins under a new license-policy.json key, noticeFiles, because scripts/check-licenses.ts owns licenseFiles for hash-pinned files inside installed packages (khroma) and rejects any other shape: accepted, the key is documented in the script header and the policy. The orchestrator read the generated file outside its fences: 10 sections, each with name@version, Licence: and File: lines, the two flow sections carrying the provenance line, 0 CR bytes.

**Ruling 3: scaffold bytes and SEC-52.** The W4-01 verifier declined to judge whether the brief's byte-for-byte rule covers the create bin's scaffold output: the create cases check exit codes, stderr, file lists and package names, not the template bytes, which carry a version injected at build time. Ruling: the byte-for-byte rule binds the codemod transforms' outputs, where it is applied; the scaffold's bytes are proven by tests/dist/create-tarball.test.ts and by template-smoke (scaffold, install, tsc, build of every template from the packed tarballs), so the create cases stand as written. SEC-52 ends PARTIAL: the CLI driver, the create CLI and telemetry are covered; the remainder is SEC-02's frecency flush-on-dispose test (1.6.0). The verifier also declined to run the whole vitest file on ubuntu with Node 28 (its Linux evidence is a WSL replay on node 26.10.0, because vitest's native bindings in this checkout are win32 builds): accepted, the dist-tests job on ubuntu-26.04 with Node 28 runs the file in CI.

**Ruling 4: accepted deviations and declines of the wave.** W4-01 generated the four shim dry-run fixtures with a temporary env-gated write hook, then removed it (the fixtures differ from the strict ones only by the coverage/ and gen/ blocks LEGACY_EXCLUDES keeps and the summary line; the WSL replay found them byte-equal): accepted. W4-03 spawned npm without a shell instead of the brief's shell command string (a shell would re-parse the temp paths): accepted; it did not check publish dates of the 96 resolved transitive packages, the release owner reads the tree the first-publish run prints (owner-gated note). W4-02b's scanner does not recognise regex literals and its comment says so as a possible false negative, accepted because rolldown's chunks are not expected to hold one and the pack gate runs on the real dist. W4-04b's Windows test cannot go red (taskkill /T already kills the tree), so the POSIX evidence is a standalone WSL run of run(); accepted, with the WSL proof outside CI recorded as a follow-up. The W4-02 verifier's two minors and the W4-04 follow-up were fixed in the same wave rather than deferred.

**Ruling 5: the W4-04b regression (DT-70) and the fix rounds c and d.** The wave-4 ledger judge (wf_f73d1758-505, an opus-verifier reading a6cf620 beside the running wave 5) found that W4-04b's killTree sends process.kill(-pid, 'SIGKILL') on POSIX and ignored ESRCH while the `next start` server spawned in consumer-smoke.ts's main was not detached: the group kill hit ESRCH, the error was swallowed and the server survived, holding its port and the script's event loop (a WSL probe: non-detached child, kill(-pid) gives ESRCH, child alive); before a6cf620 child.kill('SIGKILL') on the direct child had stopped it. Ruling: fix it in this wave, not defer it. W4-04c (a sonnet-worker, its diff read by the orchestrator; 631eeee) exports spawnServer (detached: !IS_WINDOWS, piped stdio) for the served app and gives killTree an ESRCH fallback to child.kill('SIGKILL') when the child is alive (exitCode and signalCode both null), success only when the child is already gone, rethrow for anything else; W4-04d mirrors the fallback in template-smoke.ts and exports killTree for its test. On win32 none of the new tests can go red (taskkill /T kills any tree), so the evidence is WSL Ubuntu-26.04 with node 26.10.0 through standalone scripts importing the exported functions: spawnServer plus killTree {alive: true} before the fix and {alive: false} after; the run() grandchild {timedOut: true, elapsed: 1507, alive: false}; the non-leader fallback {nonLeaderAlive: false}; the two smoke test files 43 passed on Windows. CI then showed the regression live and the fix (the CI paragraph above: the two Next consumer jobs at their 25-minute timeout on a6cf620, green again on c88d186). The judge's other extras: the overlapping-range highlight cases it proposed as a new row are SEC-43 and RA-44 (OPEN, 1.9.0), so no row was filed; the File: path printed by the two pinned notice sections became W4-06c (aaed1da: a pinned section states the source URL, the fetch date and the reason once and names no repository path, since the notices ship in the tarball where tests/package/notices does not exist; the regenerated file changed 2 insertions and 6 deletions, the two flow sections only); the dist-tests comment at ci.yml:161, which named six of the ten files in tests/dist, became DG-129 (56fac86, W5-01b). CV-25's remaining clause (templates/shared/vite.config.ts type-checked by no tsconfig) became W5-02, added to the plan (plan-1.5.0-items.json, backup plan-1.5.0-items.before-w502.json) with create-vite 9.3.4's react-ts template as its ground (downloaded into its own scratch directory and read, never executed), and it ran in a git worktree detached at a6cf620 so that it could not collide with wave 5's gate on the main checkout.

A hang still fails: every CI job carries timeout-minutes. Proven by run 42013071098 on c88d186 (every matrix green, the windows-2025 Node 28 lane included).
